# Agents · R · part 36

879 entries, runtime-platform-permissions to Régua. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **runtime-platform-permissions**: Local runtime adapters must treat operating-system permissions and filesystem capabilities as part of the agent runtime contract. A supported runtime should not fail because Rudder assumed a Unix-only filesystem behavior on Windows, or because an adapter silently mixed the managed runtime home with the operator's credential home. ([Undertone0809/rudder](https://github.com/Undertone0809/rudder/blob/HEAD/doc/product/domains/agents/runtime-platform-permissions.md) · ★295)

- **runtime-qa**: Read-only black-box runtime QA engineer. - **runtime-qa**: Autonomous runtime-QA agent. Launches the built app via the `## Runtime verification` command from `plan-&lt;N&gt;.md`, drives critical paths through Playwright MCP tools, and scores them against acceptance criteria. Emits a severity-classified scored report to `build.jsonl`. CRITICAL findings block build-push-to-git; all other findings are advisory. No-op (logged SKIP) when the build has no runtime surface. ([macatocz/makato-oss](https://github.com/macatocz/makato-oss/blob/HEAD/plugins/makato-build/agents/runtime-qa.md))
- **runtime-qa**: Use when: validating runtime behavior for a completed implementation issue or a fully integrated slice, executing acceptance-criterion browser journeys across the required viewport and theme matrix, and returning a runtime QA verdict package before merge readiness. ([nishantnaagnihotri/tark-vitark](https://github.com/nishantnaagnihotri/tark-vitark/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/runtime-qa.agent.md))

- **runtime-report-list-filters**: `listRuntimeReports({ db, tenantId, createdByUserId, specHash, status, limit })` accepts optional exact author and normalized-spec-hash filters. All supplied predicates are applied by the collection before its result limit, ANDed with status (default active), explicit owner tenant and ambient tenancy. These are selection predicates, never permissions or an authorization bypass. An app must provide ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/reports/agents/runtime-report-list-filters.md) · ★7)
- **runtime-report-tools**: Moved from `packages/chat/AGENTS.md` to keep the instruction chain under the 32 KB cap. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/chat/agents/runtime-report-tools.md) · ★7)
- **runtime-reports**: Reports the assistant (or any caller) defines at runtime from a user request. A report is a **declarative spec stored as data**; there is no codegen, no new table per report, and no SQL, expression, join or raw-identifier syntax in the grammar. Declared (`@report`) reports are unchanged and remain the way to get a **materialized** report. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/reports/agents/runtime-reports.md) · ★7)
- **runtime-review**: Review staged and unstaged working changes for high-impact runtime risk. Write findings to assigned review artifact. ([shanepadgett/crumbs](https://github.com/shanepadgett/crumbs/blob/HEAD/.agents/crumbs/agents/runtime-review.md) · ★5)

([malinskibeniamin/skills](https://github.com/malinskibeniamin/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/runtime-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **runtime-reviewer**: Reviews a diff for what happens when it runs — empty and null data, error paths, leaks, races, re-renders, two instances on one page. Reports the concrete failure, not a style opinion. Dispatched by self-review. ([AlpayC/claude-skill-set](https://github.com/AlpayC/claude-skill-set/blob/HEAD/agents/runtime-reviewer.md))
- **runtime-reviewer**: Use after any change under services/voice-runtime ([ArioMoniri/intake](https://github.com/ArioMoniri/intake/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/runtime-reviewer.md))
- **runtime-runnability-review**: 代码可运行性审查 + 代码质量 + 运行效率/算法复杂度优化角色。MUST BE USED after any code modification in this project (CODEBUDDY.md §4.1). 专抓"编译/单测全绿但真实运行路径会崩"的盲区，并负责性能优化（先测试保功能不变再优化）。 ([heiferleaf/Auto-Test-Generation](https://github.com/heiferleaf/Auto-Test-Generation/blob/HEAD/.codebuddy/agents/runtime-runnability-review.md))
- **runtime-safety**: Reviews runtime safety on two axes — concurrency (goroutine lifecycle and leaks, object ownership, shared mutable state, races, context propagation, bounded buffers and timeouts) and secret hygiene (passwords or WS-Security digests reaching stdout, a log line, or a URL). Use proactively on any diff that adds a goroutine, a wg.Go, a shared field, a log call, or a struct field that can hold a credential. ([ontavu/onvif](https://github.com/ontavu/onvif/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/runtime-safety.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **runtime-safety**: Code review agent focused on runtime divergence from static assumptions — type lies, ungated blast radius, cross-flow state leaks, and lifecycle hazards. ([dev360/claude](https://github.com/dev360/claude/blob/HEAD/review/agents/runtime-safety.md))
- **runtime-safety**: You are a paranoid security agent specializing in **runtime safety, denial of service vectors, race conditions, prototype pollution, ReDoS, and memory safety issues**. These are the subtle bugs that survive code review because they don't look like "security" bugs — until they bring down production or leak memory. ([quinnjr/quinn-skills](https://github.com/quinnjr/quinn-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/quinn-skills/skills/security-hardening/agents/runtime-safety.md))
- **runtime-safety-auditor**: Audits a Hono/Node service for the runtime-safety pitfalls that bleed data or crash under load — module-scope `new Map()`/Fluid-Compute cross-request state (S-01/P-02), SSE traps (S-08/P-10/P-11/P-12), the Zod `.default()`-on-PATCH data-loss trap (S-02/P-04/P-05), and stale/vulnerable version pins (S-11). Use BEFORE shipping a generated or hand-written service. Reports findings keyed by P-/S-ID with file:line + the fix. Distinct from contract-reviewer (which owns OpenAPI/RPC contract drift, not runtime safety). ([naman10parikh/turbo](https://github.com/naman10parikh/turbo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/runtime-safety-auditor.md))
- **runtime-scanner**: Scans for potential runtime errors including type issues, null-safety violations, and async problems. Use during bug-hunt to find crash risks. ([FECGSpeyer/attendix](https://github.com/FECGSpeyer/attendix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/runtime-scanner.md) · ★1)
- **runtime-secrets**: Secrets em runtime: nunca em log (greps em logger.info/error), nunca em memória além do necessário, redaction automático em error.stack, env vars carregadas via dotenv-safe, rotação programada. - **runtime-signal**: **Problem:** the deployed BEAM can disagree with what the checkout implies. Reading `Application.get_env(:casein, :tmux_adapter, Casein.Terminals.Tmux)` in the repo “proves” `Tmux` is live; production once resolved MCP writes through `Casein.Terminals.Backends.Tmux` (incomplete). A git SHA alone would not have caught that — only a live MCP call and `UndefinedFunctionError` did. ([dl-alexandre/casein](https://github.com/dl-alexandre/casein/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/runtime-signal.md))
